Painted ground, without the dark outline.

Three immutable content sets, actual GLBs, identical studio settings. Original → initially delivered paint → bounded pixel-only correction. Not actual-game acceptance. RX6800 findings motivate this change; the successor must land before the separate game task re-pins and judges it.

Diagnosis, judgments, costs and reproduction · All image/profile/hash evidence · Main-only successor handoff · Original/correction pairs, foundation and unchanged overlays/Archery

The first six grids deliberately repeat identical orientations: smaller motifs and no dark uniform edge ring, not a promise that one immutable image can become non-repeating. Cards display single-tile views at roughly 260px occupied width and six-cell views at roughly 100px per tile on a wide desktop, spanning the retained game close/overview sizes. Click for native pixels. These are scale diagnostics, not calibrated Godot camera screenshots. Settings and every GLB/image hash are below each card.
